(c=3, 6=6) to (e=5, 8=8).

Delta rank = 8 - 6 = 2.

Delta rank = |6 - 2| = 4.
Since Delta col == Delta rank, it's a diagonal move.
So the queen at g2 can indeed capture the queen at c6.

Comparison:
- `e8f8`: King avoids check, but white queen remains on the board.
- `e8e7`: King avoids check, but white queen remains on the board.
- `g2c6`: Captures the white queen, removes the check, and gives black a huge material advantage.
